    1.   一般資料                         1. GENERAL INFORMATION
    大新銀行集團有限公司（ 「本公司」）乃一間銀            Dah Sing Banking Group Limited (the “Company”) is a bank
    行控股公司。其主要附屬公司大新銀行有限               holding company. Its principal subsidiary is Dah Sing Bank, Limited
    公司（
      「大新銀行」）為香港持牌銀行。本公司連             (“DSB”), which is a licensed bank in Hong Kong. The Company
    同其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）提供銀行、金融            together with its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”) provide
    及其他相關服務。                          banking, financial and other related services.

    本公司之直屬及最終控股公司，大新金融集               The immediate and ultimate holding company is Dah Sing
    團有限公司，為香港一間上市公司。                  Financial Holdings Limited, a listed company in Hong Kong.

2.   未經審核之財務報表及會計政策（續）              2. U N A U D I T E D F I N A N C I A L S TAT E M E N T S A N D

編製基準及會計政策（續）                        Basis of preparation and accounting policies (Continued)

（甲）本集團已採納之新及經修訂準則                   (a) New and amended standards adopted by the Group

     以下對會計準則及香港財務報告準則                   The following amendments to accounting standards and
     （以下統稱「香港財務報告準則」       ）的修訂         Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (hereinafter
     適用於 2025 年 1 月 1 日或之後開始的年度         collectively referred to as “HKFRS”) that became applicable
     報告期間：                              for annual reporting periods commencing on or after 1
                                        January 2025:

     (i)   香港會計準則第21號的修訂－缺乏             (i)   Amendments to HKAS 21 – Lack of Exchangeability
           可兌換性

           上述修訂要求報告企業評估一種貨                    The above amendments require the reporting entity to
           幣是否可以兌換成另一種貨幣、估                    assess whether or not a currency is exchangeable into
           算即期匯率，並在其得出某種貨幣                    another currency, and to estimate the spot exchange rate
           無法兌換成另一種貨幣的結論時進                    and to disclose when it concludes that a currency is not
           行披露。這些修訂對本集團的綜合                    exchangeable into another currency. These amendments
           財務報表並無重大影響。                        have no significant effect on the consolidated financial
                                              statements of the Group.

（乙）未採納之新及經修訂準則及詮釋                   (b) New and amended standards and interpretations not yet
                                        adopted

     若干已發布的會計準則與解釋的修訂，                  Certain amendments to accounting standards and
     於2025年1月1日起仍未生效，本集團亦無              interpretations have been published that are not yet effective
     提前採用：                              from 1 January 2025 and have not been early adopted by the
                                        Group:

     (i)   金融工具分類和計量的修訂－對香              (i)   Amendments to the Classification and Measurement of
           港財務報告準則第9號及香港財務                    Financial Instruments – Amendments to HKFRS 9 and HKFRS
           報告準則第7號的修訂（於2026年1月1               7 (effective for annual periods beginning on or after 1 January
           日或之後開始的年度期間生效）                     2026)

           香港會計師公會已修訂香港財務報                    The Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants
           告準則第9號及香港財務報告準則                    issued amendments to HKFRS 9 and HKFRS 7 to provide
           第7號，以提供關於使用電子支付系                   guidance as to when certain financial liabilities can
           統時若干金融負債何時可視為已償                    be deemed settled when using an electronic payment
           付的指引，是次修訂亦就包含可更                    system, and the amendments also provide further
           改合約現金流時間或金額的合約條                    clarifications regarding the classification of financial
           款的金融資產（包括環境、社會及管                   assets that contain contractual terms that change
           治相關或然事項）及具有若干非追                    the timing and amount of contractual cash flows,

           一步闡明。                              governance-related contingencies, and financial assets

聯營公司投資之減值虧損                     Impairment loss on investment in an associate

本集團在每個報告期末評估是否有任何跡象             The Group assesses at the end of each reporting period whether
顯示在重慶銀行的投資可能發生減值。               there is any indication that the investment in BOCQ may be
                                impaired.

於2025年6月30日及2024年12月31日，本集團對    At 30 June 2025 and 31 December 2024, the fair value of the
重慶銀行投資的公平值高於其賬面值。由於             Groupʼs investment in BOCQ was higher than the carrying value. As
該投資的服務潛力並無變動，沒有就減值或             there was no change in service potential of the investment, there
回撥作出評估。                         was no assessment of impairment or its reversal.

於2024年6月30日，其公平值低於賬面值。本         At 30 June 2024, the fair value was below the carrying value. An
集團進行了減值評估，並把賬面值調減至扣             impairment assessment was performed and the carrying value was
除出售費用後之公平值，並錄入了減值撥備             written down to the fair value less costs of disposal and recorded
16,000,000港元。於2024年6月30日，該投資的   an impairment charge of HK$16 million. The fair value of the
公平值是參考重慶銀行H股在2024年6月30日         investment as at 30 June 2024 was calculated with reference to the
的收市價計算，以公平值等級的第1級別而計            closing market price of BOCQʼs H-shares as at 30 June 2024, which
量。                              was measured at the Level 1 inputs of the fair value hierarchy.

計算大新銀行之資本充足比率並無包括該投             The calculation of DSBʼs capital adequacy does not include
資之保留盈利，惟大新銀行收取重慶銀行之             the retained earnings from this investment (the “Investment”),
現金股息除外。倘若該投資維持等於或高於             except for BOCQ cash dividend received by DSB. Provided that
初始投資成本之1,213,000,000港元，該投資之     the Investment continues to be held at or above the original cost
減值將不影響大新銀行之資本充足比率。              of the investment of HK$1,213 million, impairment made on the
                                Investment does not affect DSBʼs capital adequacy.

     27. 公平值體系                        27. FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY
     本集團使用下列反映在釐定公平值中可觀察              The Group measures fair values using the following hierarchy that
     及不可觀察參數重要性之體系計量公平值：              reflects the significance of the observable and unobservable inputs
                                      used in the fair value measurement:

     級別     內容                        Level      Descriptions

     1      相同資產或負債於活躍市場中之            1          Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for
            報價（未經調整）。本級別包括於                      identical assets or liabilities. This level includes equity
            交易所上市之權益性證券及衍生                       securities and derivatives that are listed on exchanges.
            工具。

     2      除第1級別所包括之報價外，其他           2          Inputs other than quoted prices included within Level
            資產或負債能直接（即價格）或間                      1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either
            接（即從價格導出）地可觀察之                       directly (that is, as prices) or indirectly (that is, derived
            數據，該級別包括大多數場外交                       from prices). This level includes the majority of the
            易（
             「場外交易」）衍生工具合約。                      over-the-counter (“OTC”) derivative contracts. These
            該級別之金融工具可通過具活躍                       are financial instruments with quoted prices for similar
            市場近似金融工具報價、非活躍                       instruments in active markets or quoted prices for
            市場的同等或類近的金融工具報                       identical or similar instruments in inactive markets and
            價，及通過具可觀察重要參數的                       financial instruments valued using models where all
            模型釐定金融產品的公平值。                        significant inputs are observable.

     3      資產或負債數據並非根據可觀察            3          Inputs for the asset or liability that are not based
            之市場數據（不可觀察之數據）。                      on observable market data (unobservable inputs).
            本級別包括具有大部份不可觀察                       This level includes equity and debt securities with
            部件之權益性及債務證券。                         significant unobservable components.

     本集團根據香港財務報告準則第8號《營運業             Segment reporting by the Group is prepared in accordance with
     務分項》編製分項報告。向包括行政總裁及其             HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments”. Information reported to the chief
     他執行委員會成員之主要營運決策人呈報而              operating decision maker, including the Chief Executive and other
     作為資源分配及業績評估用途之資料，乃按              Executive Committee members, for the purposes of resource
     個人銀行、企業銀行、財資及環球市場及中國             allocation and performance assessment, is determined on the
     內地及澳門之銀行業務分類之基礎來確定。              basis of personal banking, corporate banking, treasury and global
     本地銀行業務之營運表現按業務活動分析，              markets and banking businesses in Mainland China and Macau.
     而中國內地及澳門之銀行業務之營運表現按              Operating performances are analysed by business activities for
     業務機構分析。                          local banking business, and on business entity basis for banking
                                      businesses in Mainland China and Macau.

         運資金融資及貿易融資，其存款來源及                 of deposits from and the advance of loans and working
         融資客戶主要是工商業及機構性客戶。                 capital finance to commercial, industrial and institutional

         證券投資管理及本集團整體之資金運用                 management for deposit taking and lending, interest rate risk
         管理。                               management, management of investment in securities and

就編製分項報告而言，對可直接認明為各個              For the purpose of segment reporting, revenue derived from
別分項之源自客戶、產品及服務收入，將直接             customers, products and services directly identifiable with
呈報於有關分項；而分項之間的資金運用及              individual segments are reported directly under respective
資金資源所產生的收入和資金成本，按參照              segments, while revenue and funding cost arising from inter-
市場利率之轉移價格機制分配至各分項。分              segment funding operation and funding resources are allocated
項間之交易乃依據授予第三者或與第三者交              to segments by way of transfer pricing mechanism with reference
易之同類條款定價。分項間之收入或支出於              to market interest rates. Transactions within segments are priced
綜合賬內抵銷。                          based on similar terms offered to or transacted with external
                                 parties. Inter-segment income or expenses are eliminated on
                                 consolidation.

所有不同分項之直接開支將歸類於有關的分              All direct costs incurred by different segments are grouped under
項分類。間接開支及支援部門開支乃依據開              respective segments. Indirect costs and support functionsʼ costs
支性質，按耗用之時間及工作量和分項營運              are allocated to various segments and products based on effort
收入，分配至不同的分項及產品。不能合理地             and time spent as well as segmentsʼ operating income depending
分配至各分項、產品及支援部門之企業活動              on the nature of costs incurred. Costs related to corporate activities
開支，則作企業開支呈列於
           「其他」項下。               that cannot be reasonably allocated to segments, products and
                                 support functions are grouped under Others as unallocated
                                 corporate expenses.

                  NOTES TO THE UNAUDITED INTERIM CONDENSED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS



31. 債權及餘額之額外分析（續）                        31. ADDITIONAL ANALYSIS ON CLAIMS AND EXPOSURES
                                               (Continued)

（丙）按區域分析之客戶貸款及墊款總額及逾                     (c) Analysis of gross loans and advances to customers and
   期貸款                                       overdue loans by geographical area

客戶貸款及墊款之區域分析乃根據已考慮風                      Loans and advances to customers by geographical area are
險轉移後之交易對手所在地分類。一般而言，                     classified according to the location of the counterparties after
當貸款的擔保方位處與交易對手不同之區域                      taking into account the transfer of risk. In general, risk transfer
時，風險將被轉移。                                applies when an advance is guaranteed by a party located in an
                                         area which is different from that of the counterparty.

     33. 風險管理                         33. RISK MANAGEMENT
     本集團明瞭各類風險會不停蛻變的特性並透              The Group recognises the changing nature of risk and manages it
     過完善的管理架構作有效管理。                   through a well-developed management structure.

     風險管理專注於信貸風險、市場風險、利率風             Risk management is focused on credit risk, market risk, interest
     險、流動資金風險、操作風險、聲譽風險及策             rate risk, liquidity risk, operational risk, reputation risk and
     略性風險。信貸風險之產生主要源於本集團              strategic risk. Credit risk occurs mainly in the Groupʼs credit
     之信貸組合，其中包括企業和零售借貸、機械             portfolios comprising corporate and retail lending, equipment and
     和租購融資及財資和金融機構的批發借貸及              hire purchase financing, and treasury and financial institutions
     財資投資。                            wholesale lending and treasury investment.

     大部份的市場風險乃源於財資及環球市場部              Market risk arises mainly from Treasury & Global Markets Division
     （「財資部」），主要與本集團資產負債表內             (“TRD”) and is associated principally with the Groupʼs on- and off-
     及資產負債表外之買賣交易及其證券投資有              balance sheet positions in the trading book and its investment
     關。                               securities.

     利率風險指因利率的不利變動而引致本集團              Interest rate risk means the risk to the Groupʼs financial condition
     的財政狀況面臨的風險。                      resulting from adverse movements in interest rates.

     流動資金風險乃指本集團未能在不衍生不可              Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group is unable to fund
     接受損失的情況下為新增的資產融資或就到              increases in assets or meet its payment obligations associated
     期之金融負債履行付款責任。                    with its financial liabilities when they fall due without incurring
                                      unacceptable losses.

     操作風險乃因內部程序、員工及系統之不足              Operational risk is the risk of loss (direct or indirect) resulting from
     與疏忽或外來的事件而產生之直接或間接虧              inadequate or failed internal processes, people and systems or
     損之風險。                            from external events.

     聲譽風險是指由於本集團之商業慣例、營運              Reputation risk is the risk arising from the potential that negative
     誤差或營運表現而可能帶來之負面宣傳風               publicity regarding the Groupʼs business practices, operational
     險。這些負面因素不論是否屬實，均可能令              errors or operating performance, whether true or not, could cause
     客戶產生憂慮或負面看法，削弱客戶基礎及              customer concerns or negative view, decline in the customer base
     市場佔有率或導致耗費龐大之訴訟或減少收              or market share, or lead to costly litigation or revenue reductions.
     入。

     策略性風險泛指由於差劣之策略性決定、不              Strategic risk generally refers to the corporate risk that may bring
     可接受之財務表現、策略施行上失當以及對              significant immediate or future negative impact on the financial
     於市場轉變欠缺有效應變而可能對集團財務              and market positions of the Group because of poor strategic
     及市場狀況有即時或日後重大負面影響之企              decisions, unacceptable financial performance, and inappropriate
     業風險。                             implementation of strategies and lack of effective response to the
                                      market changes.

     （丁）應用金融工具策略                      (d) Strategy in using financial instruments

     本集團接受定息或浮息及不同年期之客戶存              The Group accepts deposits from customers at both fixed and
     款，並以此取得之資金投資於各種類別的資              floating rates, and for various periods, and seeks to earn positive
     產以賺取息差收入。本集團尋求透過整合短              interest margins by investing and lending these funds in a wide
     期資金及按較高利率借出較長期之款項以增              range of assets. The Group seeks to increase these margins by
     加此等息差收入，同時並保持足夠之流動資              consolidating short-term funds and lending for longer periods
     金以應付可能須付之所有到期債務。                 at higher rates, while maintaining sufficient liquidity to meet all
                                      claims that might fall due.

     本集團亦按信貸風險及市場情況，透過向企              The Group also seeks to apply its interest margins through its
     業及零售借款人貸款賺取息差，以及向客戶              lending to corporate and retail borrowers and to charge customers
     收取合理費用及佣金。此等活動風險不單涉              appropriate fees and commission, taking into consideration credit
     及資產負債表內之貸款及墊款，亦涉及本集              risk and market conditions. Such exposures involve not just on-
     團提供擔保及其他承擔，例如信用證、履約保             balance sheet loans and advances, as the Group also enters into
     證及其他保證。                          guarantees and other commitments such as letters of credit,
                                      performance bonds and other bonds.

本集團亦通過交易所及場外交易買賣包括衍            The Group also trades in financial instruments where it takes
生工具之金融工具，藉著證券、債券、貨幣及           positions in exchange-traded and OTC instruments, including
利率之短期波動賺取利潤。董事會制定交易            derivatives, to take advantage of short-term market movements
限額以控制不同程度之市場持倉風險。除指            in equities and bonds and in currency and interest rate. The
定對沖安排外，有關外匯及利率之風險一般            Board places trading limits on the level of exposures that can be

易）或利用衍生工具作對沖，藉此控制有關市           arrangements, foreign exchange and interest rate exposures
場持倉套現之現金淨值。                    are normally offset by entering into counterbalancing positions
                               (including transactions with customers or market counterparties),
                               or by the use of derivatives, thereby controlling the variability in
                               the net cash amounts required to liquidate market positions.

本集團亦應用利率掉期及其他利率衍生工具            The Group also uses interest rate swap and other interest rate
以減輕因利率變動令定息資產公平值下降或            derivatives to mitigate interest rate risk arising from changes in
定息有期負債公平值上升之利率風險。若干            interest rates that will result in decrease in the fair value of fixed
金融工具被用作公平值對沖，對沖項目之細            rate assets or increase in the fair value of fixed rate liabilities. Part
節，包括被對沖項目、金額、利率、對沖期及           of these financial instruments are designated as fair value hedges,
目的，皆於各公平值對沖項目開始時被確定            and the terms of hedge including hedged item, amount, interest
和記錄，亦於開始對沖時按預期基礎評估及            rates, hedge period and purpose are determined and documented
不時根據實際經驗及估價重新評估對沖有效            at the inception of each fair value hedge. Hedge effectiveness is
性。倘公平值對沖關係不符合對沖會計的有            assessed at inception on a prospective basis and is reassessed,
效性測試標準，則對沖會計方法將於此公平            on an ongoing basis, based on actual experience and valuation.
值對沖失效日起停止。                     Fair value hedge relationships that do not meet the effectiveness
                               test requirement of hedge accounting are discontinued with effect
                               from the date of ineffectiveness of the fair value hedge.

（戊）信貸風險                        (e) Credit risk

本集團之主要信貸風險為借款人或交易對手            The Groupʼs main credit risk is that borrowers or counterparties
未能履行對本集團之償款責任。此等責任乃            may default on their payment obligations due to the Group.
源自本集團之貸款及投資活動、以及金融工            These obligations arise from the Groupʼs lending and investment
具之交易（包括衍生工具）
           。                   activities, and trading of financial instruments (including
                               derivatives).

        就某些類別之客戶貸款，本集團亦信賴                 and vehicles. For certain types of advances to customers,

                                          acceptable credit rating.

        出貸款前將被評估。問題客戶之抵押物                 are appraised before the loan can be drawn. For property

        一次。就已收回之物業抵押品，本集團                 values are appraised at least every three months. For property

        結果。

市場風險乃指由市場上利率及價格變化而引            Market risk is the risk of losses in assets, liabilities and off-balance
致對資產、負債及資產負債表外持倉之虧損            sheet positions arising from movements in market rates and
風險。                            prices.

各類交易之市場風險均由董事會、風險管理            Market risk exposure for different types of transactions is managed
及合規委員會及財資及投資風險委員會按董            within various risk limits and guidelines approved by the Board,
事會授予之權力所核准之各項風險限額及指            the RMCC and the TIRC under the authority delegated from
引內處理。風險限額按組合層面以及各產品            the Board. Risk limits are set at the portfolio level as well as by
及不同風險類別設定。該等限額綜合包含了            products and by different types of risks. The risk limits comprise
名義金額、止蝕限額、敏感性及運用市場風險           a combination of notional, stop-loss, sensitivity and value-at-risk
數值（
  「市場風險數值」）之監控。所有涉及市場          (“VaR”) controls. All trading positions are subject to daily mark-to-
風險的交易持倉需要每日按市值入賬。集團            market valuation. The Risk Management and Control Department
風險部之風險管理及監控部（ 「風險管理及監          (“RMCD”) within the GRD, as an independent risk management
控部」）乃一個獨立之風險管理及控制部門，           and control unit, identifies, measures, monitors and controls the
負責比較風險和已審批限額，以識別、計量、           risk exposures against approved limits and initiates specific actions
監控及管理該等風險及提議具體行動去確保            to ensure positions are managed within an acceptable level. Any
持倉被限制在可接受水平內。任何不符合限            exceptions have to be reviewed and sanctioned by the appropriate
額情況均須依據有關政策及程序經合適管理            level of management of TIRC, RMCC or the Board as stipulated in
層－財資及投資風險委員會，風險管理及合            the relevant policies and procedures.
規委員會或董事會審查及批准。

大新銀行之附屬公司澳門商業銀行及大新銀            BCM and DSB China, which are subsidiaries of DSB, run their
行（中國）根據其一套自定限額和政策及在大           treasury functions locally under their own set of limits and policies
新銀行設定之市場風險管理框架內執行其本            and within the market risk management framework set by DSB.
行之財資活動。大新銀行之風險管理及監控            The RMCD of DSB oversees the market risk arising from the
部監察及管理源自澳門商業銀行及大新銀行            treasury operations of BCM and DSB China to ensure that the risk
（中國）財資營運之市場風險，確保已實施的           controls put in place are commensurate with their businesses and

一致。

        (i)   源自交易賬之市場風險                  (i)   Market risk arising from the trading book

        本集團之交易賬內，在外匯、債務證券、                In the Groupʼs trading book, market risk is associated with
        權益性證券及衍生工具之交易持倉中存                 trading positions in foreign exchange, debt securities, equity
        在市場風險。                            securities and derivatives.

              (1)       市場風險計量方法                (1) Market risk measurement technique

              作為市場風險管理，本集團使用各                   In the management of market risk, the Group measures
              種業界普遍採用之方法計量市場                    market risks using various techniques commonly used
              風險及控制市場風險於設定之風                    by the industry and control market risk exposures
              險額度範圍內。主要用於計量及監                   within established risk limits. The major measurement
              控市場風險之計量方法概述如下。                   techniques used to measure and control market risk are
                                                outlined below.

                    •    市場風險數值                     •   Value-at-risk

                    本集團應用市場風險數值法，                   The Group applies a VaR methodology, which
                    其乃一種以統計為基準就持作                   is a statistically based estimate, to measure the
                    交易用途組合因市場不利變化                   potential loss of its trading portfolio from adverse
                    預計潛在損失。其表明本集團                   market movements. It expresses as the maximum
                    於某個置信水平可能損失之最                   amount the Group might lose given a certain level
                    大數額，就一日持倉期之基準                   of confidence, which for the Group is 99% for a one
                    作推算，本集團之置信水平為                   day holding period. There is therefore a specified
                    99%。因此存在明確的統計概                  statistical probability that actual loss could be
                    率，實際損失可能比市場風險                   greater than the VaR estimate. Hence, the use of VaR
                    數值之估計數為大。因此採納                   does not prevent losses outside the VaR limits in the
                    該方法並不能避免當市場狀況                   event of extreme market movements.
                    發生重大變化時超逾此等額度
                    之損失。

                    市場風險數值模型假設某個持                   The VaR model assumes a certain “holding period”
                    倉期（就本集團而言為一天）直                  (one day in the case of the Group) until positions
                    至結束持倉。市場風險數值亦                   can be closed. It is calculated based on the current
                    依據持倉之現時市值、市場風                   mark-to-market value of the positions, the historical
                    險因素過往在一個曆年週期之                   correlation and volatilities of the market risk factors
                    相互關係及波幅，使用一種歷                   over an observation period of one calendar year
                    史模擬方法計算。                        using a method known as historical simulation
                                                    approach.

                    本集團藉著回顧測試交易賬之                   The VaR model is continuously validated by back-
                    市場風險數值結果，持續確認                   testing the VaR results for trading positions. All back-
                    市場風險數值模型之有效性。                   testing exceptions are investigated and back-testing
                    所有回顧測試的偏差予以調查                   results are reported to senior management.
                    及向高層管理人員匯報。

               (1) 市場風險計量方法                                    (1) Market risk measurement technique

               在董事會及其授權之委員會設立之                                 Within the risk management framework and policies
               風險管理框架及政策中，設定了不                                 established by the Board and its delegated committees,
               同的額度、指引及管理層行動觸發                                 various limits, guidelines and management action
               額，藉此控制本集團銀行賬中有關                                 triggers are established to control the exposures of the

     （丑）金融資產及負債之公平值                   (l)   Fair values of financial assets and liabilities

     公平值受限於須由董事會負責確保本集團有              Fair values are subject to a control framework that the Board is
     適當的估值管治及控制程序之控制框架。董              held responsible for ensuring proper valuation governance and
     事會授權財資及投資風險委員會監管金融工              control processes of the Group. It delegates the responsibility for
     具之估值程序。估值由風險管理及監控部和              overseeing the valuation process for financial instruments to the
     獨立專業合資格估值師（如適用）獨立地進              TIRC. Valuation is performed independently by RMCD and where
     行，而估值結果乃定期驗證，確保公平值計量             appropriate, by independent and professionally qualified valuers
     過程之完整性。                          and the valuation results are periodically verified to ensure the
                                      integrity of the fair value measurement process.

     金融工具之公平值乃在目前市場情況下市場              The fair value of financial instruments is the price that would be
     參與者於計量日進行之有序交易中出售資產              received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly
     所收取或轉移負債所支付之價格，不論該價              transaction between market participants at the measurement date
     格為直接可觀察或使用估值方法估計。                under current market conditions regardless of whether that price is
                                      directly observable or estimated using a valuation technique.

     凡金融工具之報價隨時且定期由交易所、交              A financial instrument is regarded as quoted in an active market
     易商、經紀人、行業組織、定價服務及監管機             if quoted prices are readily and regularly available from an
     構發佈，則被視作為活躍市場報價之金融工              exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing service or
     具。於活躍市場之報價為公平值提供最可靠              regulatory agency. A quoted price in an active market provides the
     之證據，並須於可獲得時使用。倘金融資產或             most reliable evidence of fair value and shall be used whenever
     金融負債有買入價及賣出價，本集團將採用              available. If a financial asset or a financial liability has a bid price
     買賣差價中在該等情況下最能代表公平值之              and an ask price, the price within the bid-ask spread that is most
     價格。                              representative of fair value in the circumstances is used by the
                                      Group.

     倘金融工具之可觀察市場報價未能直接獲               Where observable market quotation of financial instruments is
     得，本集團利用合適及獲廣泛認可之估值方              not directly available, the Group estimates the fair value of such
     法估計該等金融工具之公平值，包括現值方              financial instruments by using appropriate valuation techniques
     法及標準期權定價模型。於應用該等金融工              that are widely recognised including present value techniques and
     具之估值方法時，本集團盡最大限度使用相              standard option pricing models. In applying valuation techniques
     關可觀察依據（例如：利率、匯率、波動性、             for these financial instruments, the Group maximises the use of
     信貸息差），而盡最少限度使用不可觀察依              relevant observable inputs (for examples, interest rates, foreign
     據。例如利率掉期合約之公平值按估計的未              exchange rates, volatilities, credit spreads) and minimises the use
     來現金流之現值計算，遠期外匯合約之公平              of unobservable inputs. For example, the fair value of interest-rate
     值一般根據現行遠期匯率計算，而期權合約              swaps is calculated as the present value of the estimated future
     之公平值則按合適之定價模型計算，如Black-          cash flows, the fair value of foreign exchange forward contracts
     Scholes模型。                       is generally based on current forward rates and the fair value of
                                      option contracts is derived using appropriate pricing models, such
                                      as Black-Scholes model.
